What is seen, heard, or felt is inspected, accepted as credible, and finally made manifest through proof.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Joint, Socket, and Sealed Vessel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_19df590e5919fb7c8ca2","text":"A stable form depends on enclosing edges, matching sockets, central support, or material worked into a firm structure.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Sensing, Assent, and Verification","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1c1e73b258b6673a4105","text":"A person detects something by sight, sound, or inward sensation, then confirms it as a trustworthy report or established truth.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Suitable Pasture and Full Animal Condition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1e4bd6a57b5d2e044890","text":"Fitness emerges from a match between setting and participant. Suitable pasture supplies abundance, care and feeding complete the animal's condition, and that bodily completion becomes dependable service.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Lineage, Clan, and Affiliation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1e7796d540c5bdb03e47","text":"Belonging is structured by return to an origin. Lineage provides the backward path, clan gives it a collective body, and clientship marks a lower but still connected position within the social chain.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:The Human Image in the Eye","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_20e7b586b660a707f715","text":"A human perceiver is oriented by what can be sensed, reflected, faced, or aligned for action.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Articulated and Geographic Centers","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2140c2c74bd84c4fe6b0","text":"A bone joint and a mountain mass are organized around a fitted or central middle.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Joined Spans of Time","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_227c411676e87b09195b","text":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:3 both occurrences of `تَوَاصَوْا۟`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Tree, Sour Fruit, and Bitter Sap","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_229868d65a3f1ae83695","text":"The plant scene gathers trunk, fruit, seed, and extracted sap. Sourness and bitterness distinguish edible produce from medicinal yield while preserving their common botanical source.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Testament, Executor, and Surety","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_229e3cce1d2d7735a8b1","text":"103:3 `تَوَاصَوْا۟`, `ٱلصَّبْرِ`, `ٱلْحَقِّ`, `عَمِلُوا۟`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000347/B002","root_000409/B001","root_001019/B007","root_001046/B004","root_001046/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Labor Crew and Earned Provision","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_246b1cde23cd80884dd6","text":"manual laboring crew `ع م ل:B006/m01`; digging and building labor `ع م ل:B006/m02`; wage or provision `ع م ل:B004/m01`; extracted yield or revenue `ع ص ر:B007/m02`; entitlement to return `ح ق ق:B002/m02`; general loss `خ س ر:B001/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Encased Crop and Piled Food","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_24faac75dea7c71d7bd3","text":"Natural enclosure precedes human accumulation. The husk guards the crop while it grows; afterward the broad support, heap, and filled edge organize it as stored provision.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Conviction Realized as Sound Action","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2b9e49e3b9ebb5d9c885","text":"A person assents to truth, turns that assent into deliberate good action, and thereby opposes loss with soundness.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Rider, Bow, Spear, and Working Limbs","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2c44874afa3484f4211f","text":"103:2 `إِنسَٰنَ`; auditory apprehension `ء ن س:B002/m02`; inward sensing `ء ن س:B002/m03`; deliberate inspection `ء ن س:B002/m04`; confirmation of report or promise `ء م ن:B002/m01`; establishment of truth by proof `ح ق ق:B005/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Lineage, Clan, and Affiliation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2ec2a05b475fb2d54207","text":"Continuity joins adjacent spans and carries collective identity through lineage, clan, and affiliation.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Transaction, Capital, and the Hard Return","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2fe3e390be5445ea40ce","text":"A dealing places capital and proprietary claims at risk, ending either in retained value or an unprofitable return.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Steadfastness Through Ordeal and Toil","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_33f84129a1d23efe1a1a","text":"Patience is rendered as controlled continuation under conditions that offer neither comfort nor an easy exit. Cold, forced pace, and toil make the internal act of holding oneself physically legible.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Contested Right Reconciled by Deliberation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_347757163b0f5cc55a1c","text":"103:2 `إِنسَٰنَ`; 103:3 `ٱلْحَقِّ`, `ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ`, and both occurrences of `تَوَاصَوْا۟`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Reciprocal Counsel as Communal Steadiness","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_38038a0e4779dc954dcc","text":"103:2 `إِنسَٰنَ`; 103:3 `ءَامَنُوا۟`, both occurrences of `تَوَاصَوْا۟`, and `ٱلصَّبْرِ`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Clan and Noble Stock","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_385d02cd3939f6e5f70b","text":"A named descent group locates its members within inherited social stock.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Named Person and Intimate Circle","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_38b5efc891a86b3f044d","text":"A lexical name locates identity by attaching it to a person, an intimate circle, a place, or inherited stock.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Layered Rain-Bearing Cloud","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_3f51012134579ab9a6d7","text":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:3 `ٱلصَّبْرِ`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Conviction Realized as Sound Action","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_3fb3d310c948d6402940","text":"Conviction is not left as an inward state.
